# Source Scripts

Generated directly from `data/dialogues.json`. The JSON bank is the release source of truth.

Speaker policy: **English = Australian officer/service professional; Cantonese = immigrant/community client.**

## D001 — Applying for an ABN
Topic: Business
Difficulty: Medium

**S1 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English:** 獨資經營登記要點做，商業號碼跟邊樣？等陣先。我記低咗。

**S2 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese:** I have opened the record about a new sole-trader registration. For this initial enquiry, tell me which part you need clarified first.

**S3 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English:** 新成立嘅獨資經營登記呢宗，商業號碼點搞？可以呀。

**S4 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese:** For a new sole-trader registration, use evidence that belongs to this record rather than information from another stage.

**S5 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English:** 稅務資料我有，獨資經營登記仲欠咩？咁點呀？我會跟住。

**S6 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese:** Keep the photo ID and tax details together and mark which item supports the point about Australian Business Number, or ABN; that makes the sequence easier to assess.

**S7 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English:** 獨資經營登記嗰句講錯咗，商業號碼先。清楚喇。

**S8 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese:** Before I advise you on Australian Business Number, or ABN, I need the current fact separated from earlier information. Please check that trading is planned to start on 1 September.

**S9 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English:** 商業號碼未清，獨資經營登記會唔會卡住？冇問題。

**S10 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese:** If the evidence changes the a new sole-trader registration record, correct the same matter and keep proof of receipt rather than opening a duplicate.

**S11 · Immigrant / client · Cantonese → English:** 清楚喇。

**S12 · Officer / professional · English → Cantonese:** Keep the photo ID and tax details, the Australian Business Number, or ABN record, and the written outcome together so the history is clear if the matter is reviewed.
